1a1(rgyud): @#// ༒ //bla ma dang mgon po 'jam pa'i dbyangs la gus par phyag 'tshal lo//bsod nams la mchog gnyis la gsol srung 'di//
1a2(rgyud): dus gsum la bskul kun gyi gshegs pa'i shul//skal dbyang nub gsum gyi gzhi gyur pa'i//phyag gsum gdab pa sogs bstan/
1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/ar+ya nri lam b+ha ra d+ha ra/badzra pa ni/tan tra na ma d+ha ri ni/bod skad du/'phags pa
1b2(rgyud): lag na rdo rje gos sngon can/drag po gsum 'joMs bzhes bya ba'i rgyud kyi gzungs zhes bya /bcom ldan 'das
1b3(rgyud): dpal bde ba chen po la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das bdud rnam par
1b4(rgyud): 'joms pa/bdud sde dang /lhan cig tu /'og min gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi pho brang na/thabs gcig tu
2a1(rgyud): @##//bzhugs ste;gnod sbyin gyi sde dpon chen po lag na gas rdo rje gos sngon po can/sangs rgyas gang ga'i klung gi
2a2(rgyud): bye ma snyed kyi rjes su gnang ba 'di /phyag na rdo rjes zung cig ces bka' bstsal nas/phyag na rdo rjes gsol pa/gnod sbyin
2a3(rgyud): stong phrag bye ba'i tshogs kyis yongs su bskor ba/mche ba rtsigs pa dang mtsham pa/mig me 'dag gi phung po 'dra ba/
2a4(rgyud): dbugs me lce ltar 'bar ba/'jigs pa'i nga ro can/pad ma dang chu lhas lag pa dang dpung pa brgyan pa/nor rgyas dang
2a5(rgyud): dung skyong gis dpul brgyan pa/klu nag po se ral khar thogs shing za ba/dga' bo dang nyer dga' bo 'bar ba'i/rna cha can/
2b1(rgyud): sbrul rin po che 'bar ba can gyi spyod pa na byas pa/rkang pas sbrul gdug pa 'bar ba gnan cing zab//bzhin rgyas pa/smin ma khre gnyer du sdus pa/ya sos
2b2(rgyud): ma mchu mnan pa/gshin rje'i cha byad can//lce glog ltar 'gyu ba/gsus po cho sbrul za ba/lag pa rdabs pa'i sgras 'jigs par byed pa/klu'i
2b3(rgyud): rgyal po 'dab bzangs skye rags su bcings pa/a la la zhes sgrogs par byed pa/rgya mtsho'i bzabs su dpag tshad rgyad khri zhi stong yod pa/rkang pa'i pus
2b4(rgyud): mor phyin pa/dbang po dang nyi ma'i steng na zhugs pa/klu thams cad rlag par byed pa'i//gad rgyangs chen mo/dgod pas kun tu 'khrugs par byed
2b5(rgyud): pa'i gshin rje'i gzugs can/khams gsum gang bar 'khro bar byed pa/klu rnams 'dul bar byed pa la phyag 'tshal lo//zhes byang chub sems
2b6(rgyud): dpa' phyag na rdo rjes/stod nas gsung du gsol zhes smras pa'o//de nas nam mkha' de nyid las gzhal yas khang dang /'khor lo chen po'i ting 'dzin dang /
3a1(rgyud): @#//ldan pa'i phyir;dag po chen pos 'khor sdus nas/phyag na rdo rje drag po ches /rdo rje 'khro bo drag po 'joms //rdo rje mi nub 'jigs byed che//
3a2(rgyud): rdo rje 'jigs byed sngags kyi brtso//rdo rje 'phrin las kun don mdzad//thabs 'dul 'khor lo sku che la//'byung ba lnga'i bdag nyid che/'dzes ldan long zer
3a3(rgyud): can dang ni/mdangs can sgra dbyangs 'arlung 'dzin te//skyed byed sngags kun sgrub pa la//'gums shing sdud dang sdom shing mdzad/mthar tul las rnams grub 
3a4(rgyud): pa la;thig le'i rang bzhin ma shigs la//kha sbyor nyi zla'i khro 'du las//bgyegs rigs stong phrag 'joms  Imdzad pa//'kho'i bdag po khyod
3a5(rgyud): la 'dud//sa bon rab tu 'bar ba yis//thabs dang shes rab gasreg gso mdzad//bde chen rab tu gso mdzad nas//sdug sngal nyon mongs
3a6(rgyud): 'joms la 'dud//A+oM a kha ro mu khaMd sarba d+ha ra rma d+ha ma naM//a r+ya nad pan nad twad A+oM a hUM/zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis dkyil 'khor gyi lha la stod nas 'dzuM 
3a7(rgyud): pa mdzad /bang chub sems dpa' lag na rdo rje la legs so zhes bya ba byin nas//'khor rnams spros te//bcom ldan 'das la legs so//legs so
3b1(rgyud): zhes bya ba byin te/de'i phyir gsang sngags 'di ni las thams cad grub pa/sdig pa thams cad 'joms pa drag po gsum 'dul ba 'di/gsung du gsol zhes brjod do//de nas phyag na
3b2(rgyud): rdo rjes bskor byas nas//spyan snga nas 'khor rnams dang thabs gcig tu zhugs so//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ga'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyis gsungs shing /byin gyis rlabs
3b3(rgyud): pa'i rigs sngags 'di rdug pa can 'dul ba'i phyir brjod par bya'o//tad ya thA/b+ho b+ho phan na ga a ti pa te ya/I dan ni lam b+ha ra d+ha ra sha ri ram badzra pa ni hi d+ha yan/ya sa ma ti tra ma te ud sa
3b4(rgyud): d+ha naM/pa b+hi shud d+he ma ta da ya tha/b+hud d+ha b+hud d+ha b+ha b+hud d+ho//ma hA tri tri ta ha ra ha ra na ga nan/pra ha ra na ga nan/tshin d+ha tshin d+ha hri da ya ma ni/b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha na ga d+ha ma ni/b+hi si ma ta
3b5(rgyud): na ga sha ri ra ni/b+hi swo ta ya hu phaT/na ga na ya ni ta na ya thA hUM phaT/na ga b+hi d+hi re ya hUM phaT/ni li bi sa na ya phaT/a tri Ti a ta pa la phaT pa ra tra ma ya phaT/kri tan da ya phaT tri tan ta
3b6(rgyud): ru ba ya phaT/ra sha na ya phaT/tra sa na ya phaT/ku sman Ni dod sa da ya phaT/pre ta b+ha sha tsa pi na sha ka ra ya phaT/yag k+Sha a pa ra smra ra b+hi na sha ka ra ya phaT/ha sa ha sa d+ha ra d+ha ra/ma ra ya ma ra ya
3b7(rgyud): na sha ya ni sha ya/swa rba big nan b+hig nan b+hi na sha ka ra ya phaT /sa rba sha kruM sang g+ha ta bi na sha ka ra ya phaT/ni lam b+ha ra d+ha ra badzra pa ni rad na pa ya ti swa hA//de'i cho ga ni gnod sbyin gyi
4a1(rgyud): @##//gnod sbyin gyi gdon gyis zin pa/sngags kyis btab ste gtor na/gtong bar 'gyur ro//klu zlog par byed na/smag shad kyi lcug mas bal po se'u 'i shing gi lcug
4a2(rgyud): mas bya'o//yi dgas dang sha za zlog par 'dod na skud pas bya'o//rims nad med par byed pa dang /zhogs phyed na ba dang /yi gam chus pa la ni skud pa sngon pos bya'o//nor 'dod
4a3(rgyud): na zhag gcig bsnyung ba byas te/spos kyis bdugs ni rmi lam du dge ba dang mi dge ba'i don thams cad ston la/mchod rten sku gdung dang ldan pa'i drung du//me tog sngon po la sogs pa
4a4(rgyud): la sngags bstang btab na/gser gi gdong tse bdun stong sbyin par byed do//rgyas btab pa gral bar 'dod na/thal bas bya'o/sgo lcags dbye bar 'dod na nyungs kar
4a5(rgyud): gyis bya'o//'jigs pa thams cad las srung bar ni/mar rnying pa 'am thal ba 'am nyungs dkar kyis bya'o//nad kyis 'jigs pa thams cad la ni/skud pa sngon
4a6(rgyud): po la srung ba'i 'dud pa byas te 'gul du tags na/srung ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro//byis pa gdon gis nyen pa thams cad la ni//mtshon sna lnga'i skud pa 'gul du gtags na/srung
4a7(rgyud): ba'i mchog tu 'gyur ro//tshom rkun zlog par 'dod na sas bya'o//dug la ni rtsa ku shas byab par bya'o//bdag srung ba la ni yid kyis sas chus bya'o//gzhan srung ba la
4a8(rgyud): ni skud pa sngon pos bya'o//las de dag thams cad las sngags zlos pa ni/gcig gam gsum 'am/lan bcu bzhi 'am nyi shu rtsa gcig gam/rgya rtsa brgyad zlas par bya'o/
4b1(rgyud): dpyas na 'grub par 'gyur ro//de skad ces bka' stsal pa nas/byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje dang /bdud 'khor dang bcas pa rjes su yi rangs ste/stan la 'khod do//bcom ldan
4b2(rgyud): 'das dpal phyag na rdo rje drag po gsum gar 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i rgyud rdzogs s+ho////u rgyan gyi mkhan po ri grod pa la/mkhan 'gro ma seng ge bdong can gyis byin gyis rlabs
4b3(rgyud): pa'o des slob dpon mdzub ri pa'i rnal 'byor pa la'o//des bal po su na ma ti lo'o//bal po'i mkhan po dge ba'i blo gros lha dang /lo tsa ba dge slong chos kyi bzang pos sgyur ba'o//de la bdag dge
4b4(rgyud): slong rin cen dngos grub gyis zhus so////rgya gar skad du badzra su pu khya kro ta tha tan kra ra tsa//bod skad du/rdo rje bde 'khros kyi rgyud kyi rgyal po//sangs rgyas dang byang chub
4b5(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus cig na//bcom ldan 'das bdud las rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba/'og min gyi gnas gzhan
4b6(rgyud): 'phrul dbang byed kyi pho brang bzhugs so//de'i tshe 'khor 'dus rnams kyi dang po/sems dpa' chen po yid cher g.yos te//gus pas bkur sti dang /rim 'gro dang /phud dud dang /sti stang
4b7(rgyud): du byas te/e ma ho byams 'gon thugs rje can/'gro ba'i sems can thams cad kun/me'i sa tshig cing mtshon gyis phog mun par ma khyams shing ri bos non/rlung gis khyer ba snying re rje/
4b8(rgyud): skyabs kyi dam pa bka' stsal 'tshal//zhes zhus pas der 'gon po sku 'dar te//zhal gyi pad ma rab phye nas/spyan gyi gzi 'dangs kun la gzigs/ljags kyi dkyil nas bka' stsal pa/
5a1(rgyud): @#//skyob pa'i dam pa 'di yin gyis//rdo rje 'khros pa'i dum bu gsum pa ngas gshad kyis//legs par nyon la yid la zung ces gsungs so//rdo rje bde 'khros kyi rgyud las bdams pa/rtam
5a2(rgyud): rgyud bstan pa ste le'u dang po'i//de nas mngon par gshad bya ba/kra shis man d+ha pa legs byas la//tshul dang ldan pa'i bum pa dgod/der ni chu rtsang rnam bdun blug/gzhan yang thog gi 'bras bu dang /dngar
5a3(rgyud): gi rnam pa dang /dri'i rnam pa dang /rin po che'i mchog dang /'bru'i bye brag dang sman rnams blugs so//bum pa de'i 'gul par dar sngon pos bkris te/steng du bla bres phub la zhag go//rdo rje bde ba'i rgyud las grub
5a4(rgyud): rdzas kyi le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas mngon par bshad bya ba/sgrub byed rang rgyud dag pa yis/skyed la tshul rgyas sam yang na stus kyang rung /hUM las phyag na rdo rje ni/dbu gsum mthing
5a5(rgyud): nag phyag zhi mdzes//sku ni che zhing mtshungs pa med//zhabs kyis rgya mtsho'i nang du yang /chu srin sbal pa'i nang du rdzis/g.yas g.yon dang po cha gnyis na/rdo rje gsor zhing dril chen 'du'//
5a6(rgyud): mtha' gnyis 'dug pa zhal du 'dren/dbu gsum g.yas dkar rig lnga'i rgyan/dbu g.yon dmar po sbrul gis mdzes/zhal dbus mthing nag lcags chu can/steng du mkha' lding tshang bar sprul/dpa' bo
5a7(rgyud): 'dug pa dpung pa'i rgyan/rin can lhun po ri rab la stegs byas zhum pa med par zhugs/de ltar lha dang rab sbyar nas/de nyid stan pa'i yan lag gis/rang lus mtshams su gzhug par bya/rdo rje bde chen
5a8(rgyud): gyi rgyud las/lha sgom pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas mngon par gshad bya ba/sgo gsum sbyor ba shes pa yis//gsang ba'i snying po 'bum du zlas/oM badzra tsan d+ha sarba du stan ta ka ha na d+ha ha
5b1(rgyud): mtshungs su//bsod nams rgya dang sngags kyi rgyal po 'di ma byung mi 'byung 'byung mi 'gyur//'di zlas mngon du 'grub par 'gyur//rtag tu mtshan ma bzang po 'byung //gzhan gi sbyor ba 'od mi 'ong //rang la nges par ston par
5b2(rgyud): 'gyur//snyen pa'i rim pa du ba ma thug 'dab pas legs par rtag par bya'o//de nas gtor ma byin pa ni rje oM a hUM phaT/ces rgyun tu gtang ngo //rdo rje bde ba'i rgyud las gsang ba'i snying po dang //ltas bstan
5b3(rgyud): pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o//de nas mngon par gshad bya ba rten rdo rje 'od du 'bar//rten pa 'phrang mtshan hUM du 'bar//dmar po'i 'od zer kun tu 'phros//'jig rten ma lus sbyangs nas kyang //slar yang thig le
5b4(rgyud): nyid la 'dus//sgra dang bcas te rang gis la bar rten par 'gyur ba'i 'khro 'du bya//rdo rje bde ba'i rgyud la sa ma'i bsam gtan kyi le'u sde lnga pa'o////de nas mngon par gshad pa bya ba//mthing
5b5(rgyud): gi shu gu son po la//gser gyi 'khor lo bris pa'i steng //bsrung ba'i snying po legs bkod de//gang ming rak+sha sbyar bar gyis//de yang dri yis yongs sgos te//sangs rgyas sems can
5b6(rgyud): nyid la stim//de nyid bum par legs bzhugs la//mchod pa'i zlas brjod gsol ba tab//de nas tshes pa'i dus bzangs la//rang nga ma gzhan la btags gyur na//bar chad thams cad nyer bzhi nas//tshe dpal
5b7(rgyud): gsod nams rgyas par 'gyur//rdo rje bde ba'i rgyud las//srung ba'i le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas yang dag gshad bya ba//'gro ba dug gis gdungs pa la//gdug pa'i dbang chen la sogs pa//'tshe ba
5b8(rgyud): 'di ltar 'byung bar 'gyur//mi dka' sems ni 'dro snying 'dod//nyam chung ma thobs dang //lhas kyang ngan//yang mo ngan zhing rta snol 'ong //de ni klu'i gnod pa ste//tshor ba nas legs par bcos na zlogs//
6a1(rgyud): @#//sa srin mi la de ltar gnod//lu sbring sha 'phrig sha ro 'byung /sha pra tho ru ngo yang 'gyur/ngo yang tshor na cos pas zlogs/sa bdag gnod pas 'di ltar 'byung /gtong yang shin du ldog 'gyur te//
6a2(rgyud): na tsha zug rngu bag po 'byung //res 'go bde ba ltar yang byed//skyabs su thig le kho nar na//de yang bcos kha shes pas zlogs//gnyan gi gnod pa 'di yin te//shin tu na tsha'i tshibs kyang che//rka lag zham khus
6a3(rgyud): dri ma ldog/sha khrag lud nas mi kyang sleb//kha yo sna yo chu ser za//kha yang mi thub gnod pa za//chu bur byung na tshang yang che//tshor bas mi chog sgrib yang dgos//gnod tshogs gzhan yun mi rtag pa//phra mo tsam la sogs 
6a4(rgyud): pas zlogs//kun la sgrims te bcos pas mtsho//rdo rje bde ba'i rgyud las/gzhan gyi gnod pa rtag pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////de nas mngon par gshad bya ba//tsan dug gu gul bla 
6a5(rgyud): rtsi dang /shu dag shing kun ma za phra//gsang sngags tab te lus la byug/gu gul nya 'khrigs bla rtsi dang //sgog pa shu dag dri chen rnams//gdug pa byas te yang na kru//gser g.yu byi ru mig
6a6(rgyud): ma phug/tsan dug ma bya'i 'khrigs par byas//cangs na srung ba'i mchog tu 'gyur//rdo rje ba'i rgyud las rte 'brel rdzas kyi le'u ste dgu pa'o////rdo rje ba'i rgyud las dum bu dang po stan pa'o////
6a7(rgyud): de nas mngon par gshang ba//gdug pa thams cad 'dul ba'i phyir//drag sngags 'di dag rjod par bya'o//oM ma ra na sod sod//ma ra na bying bying //ma ra na rmugs mugs//ma ra na hal hal//ma ra na du
6a8(rgyud): ma na mos mas//ma ra na hos bying thum ril dza//mi tshig spang te drag sngags 'di//lcags ma shor bar sbubs mas drangs//bum par stims nas rtags thon nas//mthungs sam krus na dngos 
6b1(rgyud): rir ni/b+hi swo ta ya/na ga na ni/tad thA hU~M phaT/na ga bi d+ha ra na ya hU~M phaT/yi dgas thams cad zlog pa ni/ni lam b+ha sa na ya hU~M phaT/a pa ti ti ha na pa la tra ya ya phaT/kri ta na ta ru pa ta ya phaT//
6b2(rgyud): ki ta na ta pa ta ya phaT/ro sha na ya hU~M phaT/ta sa na ya hU~M phaT/ku sman do sa da na ya phaT/na ga shin rje zlog pa'o//te ta pa shi pa na sha ka ra ya phaT/yid btegs thams cad bzlog pa'o//ya sha
6b3(rgyud): a be sma re bi na sha ka ra ya phaT/gnod sbyin laswogs zlog pa'o//ha sa ha sa da ra da ra/ma ra ya ma ra ya/na sha ya/na sha ye/swa rba bi k+sha na/bi sha ka ra ya phaT/bgyegs thams cad zlog pa'o//
6b4(rgyud): sarba shag tru~M sa~M ga bi ni sha ka rA ya phaT/dge 'dun gi dag 'dul ba'o//ni lam b+ha ra d+ha ra badzra pa ni rad nya pa ya ti swahA/rdo rje be con drag po che/rdo rje 'jigs pa bsngags kyi bdag//rdo rje 'khro bo
6b5(rgyud): bgyegs kyi gshed//rdo rje thub 'jigs pa che/rdo rje las kun grub pa che/rdo rje las rnams kun mdzad pa//sngags kyi bdag po khyod phyag 'tshal//sngags kyi ngo bo khyod phyag 'tshal
6b6(rgyud): le'u dgu pa'o////de nas klu dgug pa'i sngags bstan te//rdo rje lcags skya rlung gi sbyor//bas 'grub par 'gyur//de bzhin me yis kyang ni 'dul bar nus pa yin//de ltar
6b7(rgyud): nus phyi dgu pa'i sngags ni bshad par bya/oM badzra pa ni ma tra ya k+sha ni lam ba ra na ga hri g+hi ni g+hi na hU~M phaT/a g+hu sha ya a kar sha ya hU~M phaT//bsam gtan drag po'i bzlas pa bya//bzlas pa
6b8(rgyud): 'di la rab 'bad bya//klu rigs du ma thams cad cad kun//'gug 'gyur 'di las gzhan du min//ci srid 'gugs pa'i ting 'dzin gyis khugs par gyur ba'i klu rnams kun//
16a1(rgyud): @#//zangs zhong nang (du)spyan drangs te//khro bo'i che ba brjod nas ni//dam tshig sre zhing dug rnams zhong //sman gyi mchog gis gsos nas ni//gang gnod phyogs su gshegs su gsol//
16a2(rgyud): le'u bcu pa'o////de nas gzhan yang gshad bya ste//zhi ba'i las kyis mi dul ba'i//klu zir dag sngags bstan par bya//A+o~M badzra pi ni ma hA ya k+sha//ni lam b+ha ra na na ga ha na ha na hU~M phaT/na ga da ha
16a3(rgyud): da ha hU~M phaT/na ga swo ta swo ta hU~M phaT/na ga bi swo ta ya hU~M phaT/na ga g+ha r+dz+ha hU~M phaT/na ga b+hin d+ha b+hin d+ha hU~M phaT/bsam gtan rdo rje thogs pa yis//gdul bya'i steng na dbab par bya//'og tu ma'i
16a4(rgyud): 'khor lo bsam//me dpung chen po'i ting 'dzin gis//me 'bar ro chen dkyil du gzir//nyungs dkar nyungs nag skye tsi yis//bdag cing 'phro 'o sprul pa sprul spro//spyan zan
16a5(rgyud): sna tshogs mchi sder dang /mchu sder brab cing basprul pa spro//myur du thar bar gdon mi za'//le'u bcu gcig pa'o////de nas 'phags gso sngon can gyis//nyid la nyid 
16a6(rgyud): kyis mchod pa phul ba//hU~M pha rdo rje bdug pa' mo//bdag nyid dri yis rgyas mdzad ma//badzra d+hu pe pu tsa me hU~M//hU~M rdo rje me tog ma bdag nyid yongs su kram mdzad ma//badzra bu spe pu
16a7(rgyud): tsa me hU~M//hU~M rdorje dri chab ma//bdag nyid dngos po dag mdzad ma//badzra g+han d+he pu tsa me hU~M//hrUd rje mar me ma/bdag nyid dkyil 'khor gsal mdzad ma//badzra a lo ke pu tsa me
16a8(rgyud): hU~M/ci srid thams cad mchod pa che//ci srid mchod pa bde ba che//ci srid mchod pa bla na med//ci srid mchod pa yang dag che//e ma ho/le'u bcu gnyis pa'o////
16b1(rgyud): //de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje gos sngon po can gyi rdo rje bdengs po zhes bya ba'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas yang dag gshad bya ba//sngags drug
16b2(rgyud): pa las byung ba'i//'bras bu lha'i nge nyid//bde bar 'gyur ba kho na'o//de nas bcom ldan 'dasnyon mongs pa thams cad las rnam par rgyal ba rin po rnam par lta ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs
16b3(rgyud): nas//spyan gzur gis bcom ldan 'das phyag nas rdo rje la gsal zhing 'jam pa'i gsung gis 'di skad du//rgyal po'i dbang po mnyan par gyis//khyod ni rig sngags thams cad 'chang ba'i rje//'dul dka' ba rnams
16b4(rgyud): 'dzid thabs//'dul ba'i don du mkhas par mdzod//yi ge med dang yi ge dang //mtshan ma mtshan ma las 'das pa//de nyid kyis ni gsal byed//yang dag shes nas rnam par grol//ma shes pas ni
16b5(rgyud): 'phrul pa ste//phyin phyi log ni ji lta ba'o//de skad du 'baka' stsald pa/ma thag du//phyag na rdo rjes hU~M zhes rjod pas//sa gsum pa thams cad mdar chum ste//seng ge'i rgyal po ri dgas
16b6(rgyud): la byung ba lta bu'o//de'i rjes la slar chos rnams 'byung ba skad cig ma'i rdo rje zhes bya ba//dngos po thams cad gsal bar byed pa//khams gsum las rnam par rgyal ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la
16b7(rgyud): snyoms par zhugs//snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu khams gsum na 'dul dka' ba thams cad rang 'dus te//na bu na'i shing ltar 'dar bar gyur nas//mi zad pas ni gcig la gcig
16b8(rgyud): 'di skad du//kye drogs po bdag cag ni yid shin du skrag cig lus 'dar bar 'gyur na//ci'i phyir zhes pa dang //klu'i rgyal po ba ru na zhes bya ba des 'di skad du//'o bu cag gang gis kyang so so
17a1(rgyud): //par gyur pa ma yin bas//sa'i steng na lha thams cad kyi rgyal po tshangs pa chen po zhes bya ba la zhu bar bya'o//zhes bros kyis tshangs pa'i rgyal po'i phyogs ga la ba der song

181'a7(rgyud): gyur nas/gdon de rab tu 'bros pas 'gyur //ci ste des kyangam btang na/gang gis gdan de rtod 'gyur pa y-i thabs gzhan 'dir ni sbyar par bya/sems can rnams la phan 'dod pas /dkyil 'khor du

181'b4(rgyud): 'gyur /sa yang 'thams par 'gyur ba ste/gang gi mtshan ma de dag ni /klu'i gdon du bshad pa yin /de ni gang gi bder gyur pa yi /gso ba'i thabs kyang bstan par bya;rigasngags 'tshang gis

181'b8(rgyud): ste des kyang ma btang na/gang gis gdon de rtong 'gyur pa yi/thabs gzhan 'dir ni sbyar bar bya;lig bug mig ni drang ba dang /btag nas mig skus snar yang blugs /sha te pa la gseng 'phrom dang /rgya 
182'a1(rgyud): @#//spos dang ni yungs kar dang /nag ta ma la'i dum pa'i dang /tha shing dang ni rnam pa rdun /tshos rnams de dag bswogs nas ni /me ni chu dus btso bya zhing /mar ni phul dag bzhi dag dang /ba cin phul bzhi'i

182'b8(rgyud): dbang yang skur /de nas blo ldan yid bzhungs pas /bdud rtsi thabs sbyor ldan pa yis //sbyar sman 'dis ni lus kyang sku/gu gul dang ni yungs dkar dang /'gi wang shu dag ru rta dang /sug smel ya/
